## CHUNK 2 (lines 201–400)

**Lines 201–400 Summary:**
FF enters the motor-home. Sue, Reed, Ben, and Johnny are inside. Sue describes the operational setup. Reed observes the spatial interior doesn't match exterior dimensions ("dimensional folding? mystical expansion? both?"). Ben does a tactical sweep. Johnny asks how many upgrades it took to get from a motorcycle to this.

Sue briefs the mission: Negative Zone incursion point triangulated to industrial sector ~8 miles away. Reed has dimensional containment protocols. They need combat response, containment, and potential civilian evacuation.

Reed identifies the threat as Negative Zone fauna — biological organisms, likely Annihilus's peripheral forces, not energy phenomena. 6–20 entities initially, human-scale to 15 feet tall based on previous documentation.

Reed confirms the limitation applies — Negative Zone fauna are physical organisms. Sue then offers Salvage operational security respect: "you don't have to explain your capabilities in detail."

## CHUNK 3 (lines 401–600)

**Lines 401–600 Summary:**
Salvage discloses his looting power mechanic directly to the FF: "defeat a bad guy, touch, all the mass becomes equipment. Somehow. No idea how but it has been consistent for weeks. And my base has weapon systems so that'll be nice. If we defeat a single dimensional beast and I make contact, then I can integrate it on the spot. Meaning we get increasingly provisioned every time I make contact. Fortunately, it works through gloves."

The FF react: Reed is scientifically stunned ("mass conversion, direct material transformation with perfect efficiency") but restrains himself. Sue extracts the tactical implication (compounding provisioning advantage). Ben likes the sustained combat advantage. Johnny calls it "video game loot drops but in real life" then course-corrects.

Salvage then provides a comprehensive vehicle specification list to the FF for tactical coordination.

**Vehicle Specifications (COMPREHENSIVE — first formal listing):**
- Exterior: ~40ft x 10ft x 8ft, 8 heavy-duty self-sealing wheels

- Self-repair nanites (minor structural damage, 4-6 hours)
- Weapons: Roof-mounted swivel energy cannon, defensive side turrets, smoke/chaff dispensers, energy projection arrays (demon-forged), defensive mystical wards (detectable by Doctor Strange at 3 blocks)
- Defenses: 30-foot radius force field, holographic RV disguise, lethal/non-lethal security
- Sensors: 2-mile active scan, 8-mile passive detection (Ultron-derived), multi-spectrum including mystical wavelength
- Comms: Encrypted mast, standard/military/alien/mystical frequencies
- Interior: AI-driven with manual override; dual AI cores (navigation + threat analysis); living quarters, workshop, armory, galley, medical bay, bathroom, climate control
- Power: Micro-fusion reactor + alien energy core (months of operation) + reactors drawing from demonic energy and dimensional substrate

## CHUNK 25 (lines 5001–5250)

X-Men process the threat-diversity strategy. All major characters articulate the same insight independently:

- Colossus: "Is self-reinforcing cycle. Each team becomes better at fighting their primary threats by fighting their primary threats with you."

Xavier traces Salvage's portfolio: Avengers = cosmic/terrestrial; FF = dimensional/scientific; X-Men = anti-mutant/supernatural. Fury: "Multi-organizational approach isn't altruism. It's optimal strategic progression. Which is fine. Aligned incentives make better partnerships than pure altruism."

Xavier authorizes regular coordination: monthly rotation, threat diversity prioritization, systematic enhancement.
Cyclops plans: 3-4 ops/month, one monthly embedding, quarterly rotation across 4 threat categories → comprehensive enhancement within one year.

Colossus says something important: "Upgrades serve mission. Mission does not serve upgrades." Xavier agrees.

Grandmaster notes at end of chunk: "He told them directly. 'I want threat diversity for enhancement diversification.' And they're not rejecting it as mercenary. They're embracing it as aligned incentives."
